Method: 提出了名为ADAPT的新型预训练范式，能够高效对齐时间序列域中数据的物理属性，从而实现混合批次预训练，克服了预训练数据在输入尺寸和通道维度上的极端差异问题。

Result: 在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行了训练，并在分类基准测试中取得了新的最先进（SOTA）性能，成功实现了在时间序列领域内同时基于广泛数据集训练模型。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了多数据集联合预训练的关键挑战，为构建时间序列领域的通用基础模型奠定了重要基础，是推动该领域发展的重要里程碑。

Abstract: Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.

</details>


### [182] [Zero-shot Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Using Tabular Prior Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08400)
*Mayuka Jayawardhana,Nihal Sharma,Kazem Meidani,Bayan Bruss,Tom Goldstein,Doron Bergman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的多变量时间序列预测框架，通过将其重构为一系列标量回归问题，利用表格基础模型（如TabPFN）实现零样本预测，解决了现有方法忽略通道间交互作用的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在表格数据任务中表现优异，但在应用于多变量时间序列预测时，大多数方法将其简化为多个独立的单变量预测子问题，忽略了通道间的交互作用。为了克服这一限制，作者旨在开发一个能够捕捉多变量依赖关系的一般性框架。

Method: 提出了一种将多变量时间序列预测问题重构为一系列标量回归问题的通用框架，使得任何具备回归能力的表格基础模型都能以零样本（zero-shot）方式解决这些问题。该方法基于TabPFN-TS骨干网络实现。

Result: 展示了使用TabPFN-TS骨干网络的实验结果，并与当前最先进的表格方法进行了性能对比，证明了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一个适用于多变量时间序列预测的通用框架，通过标量回归重构和零样本学习，有效捕捉了通道间交互，拓展了表格基础模型在时序预测领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, particularly Prior-data Fitted Networks like TabPFN have emerged as the leading contender in a myriad of tasks ranging from data imputation to label prediction on the tabular data format surpassing the historical successes of tree-based models. This has led to investigations on their applicability to forecasting time series data which can be formulated as a tabular problem. While recent work to this end has displayed positive results, most works have limited their treatment of multivariate time series problems to several independent univariate time series forecasting subproblems, thus ignoring any inter-channel interactions. Overcoming this limitation, we introduce a generally applicable framework for multivariate time series forecasting using tabular foundation models. We achieve this by recasting the multivariate time series forecasting problem as a series of scalar regression problems which can then be solved zero-shot by any tabular foundation model with regression capabilities. We present results of our method using the TabPFN-TS backbone and compare performance with the current state of the art tabular methods.

</details>


### [183] [Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08404)
*Simon Zhang,Ryan P. DeMilt,Kun Jin,Cathy H. Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RIA的新方法，通过对抗训练和标签不变的数据增强来解决协变量偏移下的分布外泛化问题，在图分类任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习实践中，训练数据和测试数据往往来自不同环境，导致分布偏移，进而引发分布外（OoD）泛化问题。特别是协变量偏移（仅输入数据分布变化，概念分布不变）在实际应用中频繁出现，影响了模型的泛化性能。

Method: 提出RIA（Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training）方法：1) 受Q-learning类比启发，执行对抗性探索以生成训练数据环境；2) 通过标签不变的对抗性数据增强来诱导新环境；3) 防止模型坍缩到分布内训练的学习器；4) 可与多种现有协变量偏移OoD方法结合；5) 采用交替梯度下降-上升算法进行优化。

Result: 在多种合成和自然分布偏移的分布外图分类任务上进行了广泛实验，证明该方法相比OoD基线方法能够达到更高的准确率。

Conclusion: RIA方法有效解决了协变量偏移下的分布外泛化问题，通过对抗性探索和标签不变数据增强的组合策略，显著提升了模型在分布偏移场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.

</details>


### [184] [KV Cache Offloading for Context-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08426)
*Andrey Bocharnikov,Ivan Ermakov,Denis Kuznedelev,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Yegor Yershov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了KV缓存卸载技术在上下文密集型任务中的表现，发现现有方法会导致显著的性能下降，并提出了一种改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存卸载评估主要集中在不需要从上下文中提取大量信息的任务上，缺乏对上下文密集型任务（需要从输入提示中查找大量信息的问题）的系统性研究。随着长上下文LLM应用需求的增长，迫切需要评估KV缓存卸载在这类任务中的实际效果。

Method: 创建并发布了Text2JSON基准测试，这是一个高度上下文密集型任务，需要从原始文本中提取结构化知识。在Text2JSON和其他上下文密集型任务上评估现代KV卸载技术在Llama 3和Qwen 3模型上的表现，并分析性能下降的原因。

Result: 在上下文密集型任务中，现代KV卸载技术在Llama 3和Qwen 3模型上均出现显著的性能下降。分析发现准确率低下的两个关键原因是：键的低秩投影和不可靠的地标机制。提出的简化替代策略在多个LLM系列和基准测试中显著提高了准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，现有的KV缓存卸载技术在处理需要大量信息提取的任务时存在严重缺陷，强调了需要对长上下文压缩技术进行全面而严格的评估，以确保其在实际应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: With the growing demand for long-context LLMs across a wide range of applications, the key-value (KV) cache has become a critical bottleneck for both latency and memory usage. Recently, KV-cache offloading has emerged as a promising approach to reduce memory footprint and inference latency while preserving accuracy. Prior evaluations have largely focused on tasks that do not require extracting large amounts of information from the context. In this work, we study KV-cache offloading on context-intensive tasks: problems where the solution requires looking up a lot of information from the input prompt. We create and release the Text2JSON benchmark, a highly context-intensive task that requires extracting structured knowledge from raw text. We evaluate modern KV offloading on Text2JSON and other context-intensive tasks and find significant performance degradation on both Llama 3 and Qwen 3 models. Our analysis identifies two key reasons for poor accuracy: low-rank projection of keys and unreliable landmarks, and proposes a simpler alternative strategy that significantly improves accuracy across multiple LLM families and benchmarks. These findings highlight the need for a comprehensive and rigorous evaluation of long-context compression techniques.

</details>


### [185] [Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08438)
*Weida Li,Yaoliang Yu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在线性空间约束下提出了改进的半值近似算法框架和首个自适应算法Adalina，实现了更优的查询复杂度和均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值及其广义半值在各类归因问题中备受关注，但精确计算需要关于玩家数量$n$的指数级效用查询，高效近似仍是一个基础且长期存在的挑战，特别是在大规模应用场景中。

Method: 基于向量集中不等式建立理论框架，系统开发了满足线性空间约束的算法，统一了OFA、无偏kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法，并提出了首个自适应线性时空随机算法Adalina。

Result: 所提线性空间算法仅需$O(\frac{n}{\epsilon^{2}}\log\frac{1}{\delta})$次效用查询即可保证$P(\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\varphi}}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{2}\geq\epsilon)\leq\delta$，Adalina算法理论上实现了改进的均方误差，所有理论发现均经实验验证。

Conclusion: 该理论框架显著提升了现有无偏随机算法的查询复杂度，明确了成对采样何时有益，为大规模半值近似计算提供了高效且可自适应优化的解决方案。

Abstract: The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [186] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyTuning框架，通过渐进推理增益（PRG）指标自适应融合推理蒸馏与内部反馈强化学习，在有限监督下同时提升模型准确率和置信度忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险任务中的部署暴露了置信度忠实性问题，现有联合优化无监督内部反馈强化学习（RLIF）与推理蒸馏（RD）的方法面临高质量语料稀缺、事实性过度自信和错误更新放大三大挑战。

Method: 受人类置信度从不确定到确定积累过程的启发，提出渐进推理增益（PRG）指标衡量推理步骤是否逐步增强对最终答案的支持；基于此构建HyTuning混合后训练框架，以PRG式指标自适应重加权RD和RLIF，利用稀缺有监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量无标注查询实现可扩展性。

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准上的实验表明，HyTuning在有限监督下提升了准确率并实现了置信度忠实性，验证了"少即是多"的实际效果。

Conclusion: HyTuning有效解决了RLIF与RD联合优化中的关键挑战，为在有限监督资源下训练高置信度忠实性的大语言模型提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [187] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含真实维护事件和运行变化的涡扇发动机健康估计数据集，对比了稳态模型、非平稳模型和贝叶斯滤波器的性能，引入自监督学习方法建立了实际性能下界，发现传统滤波器仍是强基准，揭示了该逆问题的内在复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有稀疏传感和复杂非线性热力学特性的不适定逆问题。现有研究存在数据集不真实、对时间信息利用不充分、比较研究碎片化等问题，缺乏对真实运维约束下健康指标估计的系统研究。

Method: 构建了包含维护事件和运行模式变化的工业导向复杂度数据集；建立稳态模型、非平稳数据驱动模型和贝叶斯滤波器的基准对比；引入自监督学习方法在无真实健康标签约束下学习潜在表征，比较其下游估计性能。

Result: 传统贝叶斯滤波器仍是强基准方法；自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性，为解决该逆问题建立了实际难度下界；研究表明需要更先进且可解释的推理策略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了真实场景下的涡扇发动机健康估计基准，证明了传统方法的有效性，同时通过自监督学习揭示了问题的本质难度，为未来研究指明了方向。数据集和实现代码已开源以确保可重复性。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [188] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTVS框架，通过在线变分合成和测试时混合探索，使大推理模型能够在无标签测试数据上自我进化，无需昂贵的人工标注即可超越有监督强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习范式在专业化或新颖领域受限于高昂的标注成本；而现有测试时适应方法因从静态查询集学习，存在过拟合文本模式的风险。

Method: 提出测试时变分合成（TTVS）框架，包含两个模块：（1）在线变分合成，将静态测试查询转换为动态的、语义等价的多样化变体流；（2）测试时混合探索，在合成变体中平衡准确性驱动的利用与一致性驱动的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上TTVS表现优异；仅使用无标签测试时数据，TTVS不仅超越其他测试时适应方法，还优于在海量高质量标注数据上训练的最先进有监督强化学习技术。

Conclusion: TTVS有效解决了专业领域标注数据稀缺下的模型适应问题，证明了通过动态合成变体进行测试时自我演化的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [189] [Persistence-Augmented Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08469)
*Elena Xinyi Wang,Arnur Nigmetov,Dmitriy Morozov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) provides tools to describe the shape of data, but integrating topological features into deep learning pipelines remains challenging, especially when preserving local geometric structure rather than summarizing it globally. We propose a persistence-based data augmentation framework that encodes local gradient flow regions and their hierarchical evolution using the Morse-Smale complex. This representation, compatible with both convolutional and graph neural networks, retains spatially localized topological information across multiple scales. Importantly, the augmentation procedure itself is efficient, with computational complexity $O(n \log n)$, making it practical for large datasets. We evaluate our method on histopathology image classification and 3D porous material regression, where it consistently outperforms baselines and global TDA descriptors such as persistence images and landscapes. We also show that pruning the base level of the hierarchy reduces memory usage while maintaining competitive performance. These results highlight the potential of local, structured topological augmentation for scalable and interpretable learning across data modalities.

</details>


### [190] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习环境下对称均匀量化对航空航天预测性维护模型的影响，发现INT4量化在保持与FP32统计上无显著差异的精度同时，实现了8倍的通信成本降低，并满足FPGA硬件约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习能够在分布式航空航天机群中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽受限IoT节点上的部署，因此需要研究量化方法来平衡精度与效率。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级1-D卷积模型（AeroConv1D，9,697参数），在NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集上使用真实Non-IID客户端分区进行联邦学习训练，对比了不同量化位宽（32、8、4、2位）的效果，并进行了严格多种子评估（N=10）。

Result: INT4量化在FD001和FD002数据集上达到了与FP32统计上无显著差异的精度（p值分别为0.341、0.264和0.534），同时将每轮梯度通信成本从37.88 KiB降低到4.73 KiB（8倍减少）。INT2量化虽然在FD002上MAE较低，但NASA分数表现出灾难性的不稳定性（变异系数45.8% vs FP32的22.3%）。FPGA资源分析确认INT4满足硬件约束（DSP利用率85.5%）。

Conclusion: INT4量化是在带宽受限IoT节点上部署联邦学习预测性维护的最佳权衡点，能够在保持精度的同时显著降低通信开销并满足硬件约束；INT2量化因极端量化导致的过正则化而不可用；正确的Non-IID评估对揭示极端量化的真实操作不稳定性至关重要。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


### [191] [The Impact of Dimensionality on the Stability of Node Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08492)
*Tobias Schumacher,Simon Reichelt,Markus Strohmaier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了嵌入维度对图节点嵌入稳定性和下游任务性能的影响，发现不同方法的稳定性随维度变化呈现不同模式，且最大稳定性与最优性能并不总是一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明神经网络节点嵌入在使用相同参数和数据集、仅改变训练随机种子时会产生不同结果，但关键超参数（如嵌入维度）如何影响这种不稳定性尚未得到深入分析。

Method: 系统评估了ASNE、DGI、GraphSAGE、node2vec和VERSE五种广泛使用的方法，在多个数据集和不同嵌入维度下，从表示视角和功能视角评估稳定性，同时进行性能评估。

Result: 嵌入稳定性随维度显著变化，但不同方法呈现不同模式：node2vec和ASNE等方法在高维度时趋于更稳定，其他方法则无此趋势；最大稳定性与最优任务性能不一定对齐。

Conclusion: 嵌入维度的选择至关重要，研究揭示了图表示学习中稳定性、性能和计算效率之间的权衡关系，为超参数选择提供了新见解。

Abstract: Previous work has established that neural network-based node embeddings return different outcomes when trained with identical parameters on the same dataset, just from using different training seeds. Yet, it has not been thoroughly analyzed how key hyperparameters such as embedding dimension could impact this instability. In this work, we investigate how varying the dimensionality of node embeddings influences both their stability and downstream performance. We systematically evaluate five widely used methods -- ASNE, DGI, GraphSAGE, node2vec, and VERSE -- across multiple datasets and embedding dimensions. We assess stability from both a representational perspective and a functional perspective, alongside performance evaluation. Our results show that embedding stability varies significantly with dimensionality, but we observe different patterns across the methods we consider: while some approaches, such as node2vec and ASNE, tend to become more stable with higher dimensionality, other methods do not exhibit the same trend. Moreover, we find that maximum stability does not necessarily align with optimal task performance. These findings highlight the importance of carefully selecting embedding dimension, and provide new insights into the trade-offs between stability, performance, and computational effectiveness in graph representation learning.

</details>


### [192] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多token激活修补框架揭示了转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，可在稀疏化90-99%后仍保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转向向量是一种高效的LLM对齐技术，但目前缺乏对其内部机制的可解释性研究，即不清楚其影响哪些内部机制以及如何导致不同的模型输出。

Method: 以拒绝行为为案例研究，提出多token激活修补框架，对转向向量进行因果机制分析；对转向后的OV电路进行数学分解；基于修补结果进行稀疏化实验。

Result: 不同转向方法在同一层应用时利用功能可互换的电路；转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，对QK电路影响很小（冻结注意力分数仅导致8.75%性能下降）；OV电路分解揭示语义可解释概念；转向向量可稀疏化90-99%而保持大部分性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了转向向量生效的因果机制，表明其通过特定电路路径发挥作用，且具有高度可稀疏性，为理解和改进模型对齐技术提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


### [193] [Meta-learning In-Context Enables Training-Free Cross Subject Brain Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08537)
*Mu Nan,Muquan Yu,Weijian Mai,Jacob S. Prince,Hossein Adeli,Rui Zhang,Jiahang Cao,Benjamin Becker,John A. Pyles,Margaret M. Henderson,Chunfeng Song,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte,Michael J. Tarr,Xiaoqing Hu,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于元优化的fMRI视觉解码方法，无需微调即可泛化到新受试者，仅通过少量示例实现跨主体和跨扫描仪的通用解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉解码领域的一个核心目标是实现跨主体的通用化模型。然而，不同个体间神经表征存在显著差异，传统方法需要为每个受试者训练定制模型或单独微调，阻碍了通用模型的发展。

Method: 提出一种元优化方法，针对新受试者进行上下文学习。首先，在多个脑区通过构建刺激-响应上下文估计体素级视觉响应编码器参数；其次，构建编码器参数和响应值上下文，执行聚合功能反演，通过反转编码器进行解码。该方法无需解剖对齐或刺激重叠。

Result: 在多种视觉主干网络上展示了强大的跨主体和跨扫描仪泛化能力，无需重新训练或微调即可对新受试者进行语义视觉解码。

Conclusion: 这项工作是迈向非侵入式脑解码通用基础模型的关键一步，为解决神经表征个体差异问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visual decoding from brain signals is a key challenge at the intersection of computer vision and neuroscience, requiring methods that bridge neural representations and computational models of vision. A field-wide goal is to achieve generalizable, cross-subject models. A major obstacle towards this goal is the substantial variability in neural representations across individuals, which has so far required training bespoke models or fine-tuning separately for each subject. To address this challenge, we introduce a meta-optimized approach for semantic visual decoding from fMRI that generalizes to novel subjects without any fine-tuning. By simply conditioning on a small set of image-brain activation examples from the new individual, our model rapidly infers their unique neural encoding patterns to facilitate robust and efficient visual decoding. Our approach is explicitly optimized for in-context learning of the new subject's encoding model and performs decoding by hierarchical inference, inverting the encoder. First, for multiple brain regions, we estimate the per-voxel visual response encoder parameters by constructing a context over multiple stimuli and responses. Second, we construct a context consisting of encoder parameters and response values over multiple voxels to perform aggregated functional inversion. We demonstrate strong cross-subject and cross-scanner generalization across diverse visual backbones without retraining or fine-tuning. Moreover, our approach requires neither anatomical alignment nor stimulus overlap. This work is a critical step towards a generalizable foundation model for non-invasive brain decoding.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Blink: CPU-Free LLM Inference by Delegating the Serving Stack to GPU and SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07609)
*Mohammad Siavashi,Mariano Scazzariello,Gerald Q. Maguire,Dejan Kostić,Marco Chiesa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Blink通过SmartNIC和GPU协同卸载CPU任务，消除稳态推理路径中的主机CPU依赖，显著提升LLM推理性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统依赖主机CPU进行编排和令牌级控制，使其性能易受CPU干扰影响，限制了应用共存能力并迫使运营者预留CPU余量，导致资源利用率不足。

Method: 提出Blink端到端推理架构，将请求处理卸载至SmartNIC，通过RDMA直接将输入传输至GPU内存；使用持久GPU内核替代主机调度，实现无CPU参与的批处理、调度和KV缓存管理。

Result: 与TensorRT-LLM、vLLM和SGLang相比，Blink在隔离环境下表现更优：预饱和P99 TTFT降低8.47倍，P99 TPOT降低3.40倍，解码吞吐量提升2.1倍，每token能耗降低48.6%。在CPU干扰下，现有系统性能下降达两个数量级，而Blink保持稳定。

Conclusion: Blink成功将主机CPU从稳态推理路径中移除，实现了高性能、高能效且对CPU干扰具有鲁棒性的LLM推理服务，为数据中心LLM部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference is rapidly becoming a core datacenter service, yet current serving stacks keep the host CPU on the critical path for orchestration and token-level control. This makes LLM performance sensitive to CPU interference, undermining application colocation and forcing operators to reserve CPU headroom, leaving substantial capacity unutilized.
  We introduce Blink, an end-to-end serving architecture that removes the host CPU from the steady-state inference path by redistributing responsibilities across a SmartNIC and a GPU. Blink offloads request handling to the SmartNIC, which delivers inputs directly into GPU memory via RDMA, and replaces host-driven scheduling with a persistent GPU kernel that performs batching, scheduling, and KV-cache management without CPU involvement.
  Evaluated against TensorRT-LLM, vLLM, and SGLang, Blink outperforms all baselines even in isolation, reducing pre-saturation P99 TTFT by up to 8.47$\times$ and P99 TPOT by up to 3.40$\times$, improving decode throughput by up to 2.1$\times$, and reducing energy per token by up to 48.6$\%$. Under CPU interference, Blink maintains stable performance, while existing systems degrade by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [195] [Reduced-Mass Orbital AI Inference via Integrated Solar, Compute, and Radiator Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07760)
*Stephen Gaalema,Samuel Indyk,Clinton Staley*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向SSO计算卫星的分布式计算架构，可实现>100 kW/吨的计算功率密度，支持在单次Starship任务中部署16 MW计算能力的卫星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间计算系统在功率密度和热管理方面存在限制，传统卫星的比功率低于100 W/kg。随着大型语言模型等计算密集型应用的需求增长，需要开发高功率密度、高效散热的空间计算架构。

Method: 提出一种集成架构，将太阳能电池、散热器和计算功能集成到多个小型面板中，排列成大型阵列。利用散热器结构支撑太阳能电池，形成大型蒸汽腔散热器，使IC结温保持在约40°C。设计1-4 m²的面板尺寸以平衡热传输、计算效率和面板间通信。

Result: 比功率可达约500 W/kg（相比传统实现<100 W/kg）；单个Starship载荷可部署16 MW计算能力、150吨卫星（20 m × 2200 m网格，16000个面板）；512面板子阵列可运行50万token上下文窗口、128注意力块的推理型LLM，速度553 tokens/秒/会话，支持256个并发会话；完整卫星可支持31个子阵列，同时进行>7900次推理。

Conclusion: 该分布式计算架构通过太阳能电池、散热器和计算功能的协同集成设计，实现了高功率密度和优异的热管理性能，具备良好的可扩展性，为空间大规模计算应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a distributed compute architecture for SSO computational satellites that can potentially provide >100 kW compute power per launched metric ton (including deployment and station keeping mass). The architecture co-locates and integrates the solar cells, radiator, and compute functions into multiple small panels arranged in a large array. The resultant large vapor chamber radiator area per panel should permit ICs to operate at junction temperatures near 40*C with benefits in compute efficiency and reliability. Using the structure of the radiator to support the solar cells may also yield a specific power of about 500 W/kg compared to less than 100 for existing conventional implementations. Assuming development of custom solutions for all components, a 16 MW computation, 150 ton satellite comprising a 20 m x 2200 m grid of 16,000 panels can fit in a single Starship hold. The concept is scalable to much larger satellites with higher mass payloads or using on-orbit assembly. We consider panel sizes from 1 to 4 m2 to allow trading vapor chamber heat transport with compute efficiency and inter-panel communication. Assuming a 1 kW/panel design, 512-panel subarrays of the satellite can run a representative inference-only LLM with 500,000 token context window and 128 attention blocks, at a rate of 553 tokens/sec/session, across 256 simultaneous in-flight sessions. A full satellite could support 31 such subarrays, for >7900 inferences at a time.

</details>


### [196] [Administrative Decentralization in Edge-Cloud Multi-Agent for Mobile Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07767)
*Senyao Li,Zhigang Zuo,Haozhao Wang,Junyu Chen,Zhanbo Jin,Ruixuan LI*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AdecPilot，通过管理去中心化原则重构边缘-云端多智能体框架，实现高层策略与底层执行的解耦，显著提升了移动自动化任务的成功率、降低了云端Token消耗和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式边缘-云框架将管理中心集中于云端，边缘设备仅被动执行，导致对实时UI动态变化的认知滞后，无法及时响应界面更新。

Method: 提出管理去中心化的边缘-云多智能体框架AdecPilot：(1)云端设计器生成与UI无关的抽象里程碑；(2)双模态边缘团队实现自主战术规划与自纠正，无需云端干预；(3)引入分层隐式终止协议（HIT），强制确定性停止，防止任务完成后的幻觉问题。

Result: 与EcoAgent相比，任务成功率提升21.7%，云端Token消耗降低37.5%；与CORE相比，端到端延迟降低88.9%。

Conclusion: AdecPilot通过将管理职能从云端下沉至边缘，实现了更高效、更低延迟且更可靠的移动自动化方案，验证了边缘-云去中心化架构的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative edge-cloud frameworks have emerged as the main- stream paradigm for mobile automation, mitigating the latency and privacy risks inherent to monolithic cloud agents. However, existing approaches centralize administration in the cloud while relegating the device to passive execution, inducing a cognitive lag regard- ing real-time UI dynamics. To tackle this, we introduce AdecPilot by applying the principle of administrative decentralization to the edge-cloud multi-agent framework, which redefines edge agency by decoupling high-level strategic designing from tactical grounding. AdecPilot integrates a UI-agnostic cloud designer generating ab- stract milestones with a bimodal edge team capable of autonomous tactical planning and self-correction without cloud intervention. Furthermore, AdecPilot employs a Hierarchical Implicit Termi- nation protocol to enforce deterministic stops and prevent post- completion hallucinations. Extensive experiments demonstrate pro- posed approach improves task success rate by 21.7% while reducing cloud token consumption by 37.5% against EcoAgent and decreas- ing end to end latency by 88.9% against CORE. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Anonymous_code- B8AB.

</details>


### [197] [LogAct: Enabling Agentic Reliability via Shared Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07988)
*Mahesh Balakrishnan,Ashwin Bharambe,Davide Testuggine,David Geraghty,David Mao,Vidhya Venkat,Ilya Mironov,Rithesh Baradi,Gayathri Aiyer,Victoria Dudin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了LogAct，一种基于共享日志的Agent抽象，通过将Agent解构为状态机实现可观测、可中断和可恢复的执行，支持Agent自省以实现故障恢复、性能调试和优化，在实验中能高效正确地从故障中恢复并以仅3%的良性效用损失阻止所有不良行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的Agent能够以强大且任意的方式改变环境，但由于生产环境中的异步性和故障，难以对其执行提供保证。现有的Agent执行缺乏足够的可观测性、可干预性和一致性恢复机制。

Method: 提出LogAct抽象，将每个Agent解构为一个共享日志上的状态机。Agent行为在执行前对共享日志可见；通过可插拔、解耦的投票器在执行前可中断行为；在Agent或环境故障时能一致恢复。支持Agent自省，利用LLM推理分析自身执行历史，实现语义级的恢复、健康检查和优化。

Result: 实验评估表明，LogAct Agent能高效正确地从故障中恢复；能调试自身性能；在群体Agent中优化token使用；在代表性基准测试中，以仅3%的良性效用损失为代价阻止目标模型的所有不良行为。

Conclusion: LogAct提供了一种有效的Agent执行抽象，通过共享日志机制实现了行为可观测、可中断、可一致恢复，并借助Agent自省能力提升了系统的可靠性、可调试性和安全性，为生产环境中Agent的可控执行提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Agents are LLM-driven components that can mutate environments in powerful, arbitrary ways. Extracting guarantees for the execution of agents in production environments can be challenging due to asynchrony and failures. In this paper, we propose a new abstraction called LogAct, where each agent is a deconstructed state machine playing a shared log. In LogAct, agentic actions are visible in the shared log before they are executed; can be stopped prior to execution by pluggable, decoupled voters; and recovered consistently in the case of agent or environment failure. LogAct enables agentic introspection, allowing the agent to analyze its own execution history using LLM inference, which in turn enables semantic variants of recovery, health check, and optimization. In our evaluation, LogAct agents recover efficiently and correctly from failures; debug their own performance; optimize token usage in swarms; and stop all unwanted actions for a target model on a representative benchmark with just a 3% drop in benign utility.

</details>


### [198] [LegoDiffusion: Micro-Serving Text-to-Image Diffusion Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08123)
*Lingyun Yang,Suyi Li,Tianyu Feng,Xiaoxiao Jiang,Zhipeng Di,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Yinghao Yu,Tao Lan,Guodong Yang,Lin Qu,Liping Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出LegoDiffusion系统，通过将文本到图像生成的扩散工作流分解为松耦合的模型执行节点，实现独立管理和调度，在请求处理率和突发流量容忍度上分别达到现有系统3倍和8倍的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像服务系统将整个扩散工作流视为不透明的单体进行资源配置和管理，导致内部数据流不透明、无法共享模型、资源管理粒度粗糙，无法充分发挥集群级优化潜力。

Method: 提出LegoDiffusion微服务架构，将扩散工作流分解为松耦合的模型执行节点，实现独立管理、调度和显式管理单个模型推理，从而解锁细粒度优化，包括单模型伸缩、模型共享和自适应模型并行。

Result: 相比现有扩散工作流服务系统，LegoDiffusion能够维持高达3倍的请求率，容忍高达8倍的突发流量，显著提升系统性能和弹性。

Conclusion: 通过微服务化的架构设计，LegoDiffusion证明了将扩散工作流分解为独立可管理的节点能够有效提升服务系统的性能和可扩展性，为文本到图像生成服务提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation executes a diffusion workflow comprising multiple models centered on a base diffusion model. Existing serving systems treat each workflow as an opaque monolith, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models together, which obscures internal dataflow, prevents model sharing, and enforces coarse-grained resource management. In this paper, we make a case for micro-serving diffusion workflows with LegoDiffusion, a system that decomposes a workflow into loosely coupled model-execution nodes that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model inference, LegoDiffusion unlocks cluster-scale optimizations, including per-model scaling, model sharing, and adaptive model parallelism. Collectively, LegoDiffusion outperforms existing diffusion workflow serving systems, sustaining up to 3x higher request rates and tolerating up to 8x higher burst traffic.

</details>


### [199] [Wattlytics: A Web Platform for Co-Optimizing Performance, Energy, and TCO in HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08182)
*Ayesha Afzal,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Wattlytics，一个首个交互式浏览器端GPU计算系统决策支持工具，集成了性能建模、功耗分析和多年度TCO计算，帮助用户在不确定性下进行HPC集群设计和采购决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速计算系统日益增长的计算需求和能源足迹使设计和运营决策变得复杂。现有的采购导向计算器功能有限，缺乏集成的性能、功耗和成本分析能力，无法有效支持复杂的HPC系统设计决策。

Method: 开发了Wattlytics交互式浏览器端决策支持系统，创新性地整合了：(1)基准驱动的GPU性能扩展建模；(2)DVFS感知的分段功耗建模；(3)多年TCO分析。支持当代GPU架构（GH200、H100、L40S等）和科学工作负载（GROMACS、AMBER），提供多维度决策指标、设计空间探索、敏感性分析和协作功能。

Result: 在固定预算、固定GPU数量、固定性能和固定功耗等不同运营模式下的案例研究表明：在预算或能源约束下，优化部署的能效型GPU在整体成本效益方面可以超越更高性能的替代方案。系统能够区分成本驱动因素和风险驱动因素。

Conclusion: Wattlytics将HPC设计转变为一个充分知情且可解释的决策过程，帮助用户探索设计参数空间，在不确定性条件下实现更优的集群设计和采购决策，证明了能效优化策略的价值。

Abstract: The escalating computational demands and energy footprint of GPU-accelerated computing systems complicate informed design and operational decisions. We present the first release of Wattlytics (https://wattlytics.netlify.app), an interactive, browser-based decision-support system. Unlike existing procurement-oriented calculators, Wattlytics uniquely integrates benchmark-driven GPU performance scaling, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS)-aware piecewise power modeling, and multi-year total cost of ownership (TCO) analysis within a single interactive environment. Users can configure heterogeneous systems across contemporary GPU architectures (GH200, H100, L40S, L40, A40, A100, and L4), select representative scientific workloads (e.g., GROMACS, AMBER), and explore deployment scenarios under constraints such as energy prices, system lifetime, and frequency scaling. Wattlytics computes multidimensional decision metrics (TCO breakdown, work-per-TCO, power-per-TCO, and work-per-watt-per-TCO) and supports design-space exploration, what-if scenarios, sensitivity metrics (elasticity, Sobol indices, Monte Carlo) and collaborative features to guide realistic cluster design and procurement under uncertainty. We demonstrate selected scenarios comparing deployment strategies under different operational modes: ixed budget, fixed GPU count, fixed performance, and fixed power. Our case studies show that, under budget or energy constraints, optimally deployed energy-efficient GPUs can outperform higher-performance alternatives in overall cost-effectiveness. Wattlytics helps users explore the design parameter space and distinguish between cost- and risk-driving factors, turning HPC design into a well-informed and explainable decision-making process.

</details>


### [200] [Scheduling Coflows in Multi-Core OCS Networks with Performance Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08242)
*Xin Wang,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对多核光电路交换(OCS)网络中的协同流调度问题，提出了一种近似算法，联合优化跨核流量分配与单核电路调度，有效降低了加权协同流完成时间(CCT)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同流调度研究主要局限于单核网络环境，而现代数据中心网络已采用多核并行架构以满足带宽需求，多核OCS网络下的调度研究尚属空白。由于跨核流量分配带来的耦合关系以及单核OCS的端口独占性和重配置延迟约束，使得该问题极具挑战性。

Method: 在非全停重配置模型下（单电路重配置不中断其他电路），提出了一种近似算法框架。该算法联合优化跨核流量分配与各核内的电路调度，以最小化总加权协同流完成时间(CCT)，并提供了可证明的最坏情况性能保证。该框架也可直接应用于多核EPS（电子分组交换）网络场景。

Result: 理论证明了算法的近似比界限；基于真实Facebook负载数据的仿真实验表明，该方法有效降低了加权CCT及尾部CCT性能指标。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了多核OCS网络协同流调度的理论空白，提出的算法框架具有良好的通用性与理论保证，为现代数据中心网络的高效协同流调度提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Coflow provides a key application-layer abstraction for capturing communication patterns, enabling the efficient coordination of parallel data flows to reduce job completion times in distributed systems. Modern data center networks (DCNs) are employing multiple independent optical circuit switching (OCS) cores operating concurrently to meet the massive bandwidth demands of application jobs. However, existing coflow scheduling research primarily focuses on the single-core setting, with multi-core fabrics only for EPS (electrical packet switching) networks.
  To address this gap, this paper studies the coflow scheduling problem in multi-core OCS networks under the not-all-stop reconfiguration model in which one circuit's reconfiguration does not interrupt other circuits. The challenges stem from two aspects: (i) cross-core coupling induced by traffic assignment across heterogeneous cores; and (ii) per-core OCS scheduling constraints, namely port exclusivity and reconfiguration delay. We propose an approximation algorithm that jointly integrates cross-core flow assignment and per-core circuit scheduling to minimize the total weighted coflow completion time (CCT) and establish a provable worst-case performance guarantee. Furthermore, our algorithm framework can be directly applied to the multi-core EPS scenario with the corresponding approximation ratio under packet-switched fabrics. Trace-driven simulations using real Facebook workloads demonstrate that our algorithm effectively reduces weighted CCT and tail CCT.

</details>


### [201] [Asynchronous Quantum Distributed Computing: Causality, Snapshots, and Global Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08298)
*Siddhartha Visveswara Jayanti,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次研究异步量子分布式系统，基于经典的Chandy-Lamport快照算法设计了QGO算法来实现可分解的全局量子操作，证明了Lamport的计算因果性在量子世界（即使存在纠缠）依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分布式算法无法直接应用于量子系统，因为量子纠缠使得系统状态无法用标准的全局量子态描述来直观体现因果性。作者希望探索异步量子分布式系统，特别是实现可分解为局部操作的全局量子操作（如量子快照）。

Method: 作者首先建立了量子分布式计算的形式化模型，并形式化定义了全局操作的预期行为规范。随后基于经典的Chandy-Lamport快照算法，设计了量子分布式算法QGO Algorithm来实现可分解的全局量子操作。

Result: 成功提出了QGO算法，分析表明基于Lamport计算因果性的论证在量子世界中仍然有效，即使由于纠缠的存在，因果性无法从系统的标准全局量子态描述中直接体现。此外，提出的形式化模型和全局操作规范也适用于经典场景（如随机化算法）。

Conclusion: 证明了Lamport的计算因果性在量子分布式系统中依然成立，尽管纠缠使得量子系统的因果性不再直观，但仍可设计有效的分布式算法。这为量子分布式计算奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We initiate the study of asynchronous quantum distributed systems, focusing on the case of implementing atomic quantum global operations that can be decomposed into a collection of local operations on the components of the system. A simple example of such an operation is a quantum snapshot in which the whole system is instantaneously measured. Based on the classical snapshot algorithm of Chandy and Lamport, we design a quantum distributed algorithm to implement such decomposable global operations, which we call the QGO Algorithm. The analysis of our algorithm shows that arguments based on Lamport's computational causality remain valid in the quantum world, even though, due to entanglement, causality is not manifest from the standard description of the system in terms of a (global) quantum state. Our other contributions include a formal model of quantum distributed computing, and a formal specification for the desired behavior of a global operation, which may be of interest even in classical settings (such as in the setting of randomized algorithms).

</details>


### [202] [City-Scale Visibility Graph Analysis via GPU-Accelerated HyperBall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08374)
*Alex Hodge,Melissa Barrientos Trinanes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套将空间句法中的可视图分析（VGA）扩展至城市尺度的系统，通过压缩存储、近似算法和GPU加速实现了239倍的端到端加速，并成功处理了达23.6万个单元（48亿条边）的大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可视图分析（VGA）依赖全对BFS计算，计算复杂度高，限制了其实际应用范围仅限于小型研究区域。研究者希望突破这一限制，将VGA扩展到城市尺度的大规模空间分析。

Method: 系统结合了三项关键技术：（i）基于LEB128变长编码的delta压缩CSR存储，实现约4倍压缩率并支持超过可用内存的内存映射图；（ii）HyperBall概率距离估计器，基于HyperLogLog计数器传播，首次应用于可视图，将BFS复杂度从O(N|E|)降至O(D|E|2^p)；（iii）GPU加速的CUDA内核，采用融合解码-联合内核，通过PCIe流式传输压缩图并在共享内存中完成LEB128解码。

Result: 在使用相同可视算法（sparkSieve2）确保边集一致的前提下，系统在42,705个单元时实现了239倍端到端加速，并可在137秒内处理236,000个单元（48亿条边）的超大规模问题，远超depthmapX的实际极限。在p=10时，Visual Mean Depth的Pearson相关系数达r=0.999，中位相对误差仅1.7%。

Conclusion: 该系统成功解决了VGA方法在城市尺度应用中的计算瓶颈，使从业者能够处理远超传统工具能力范围的大规模空间配置分析，同时保持了与标准工具的高度一致性和准确性。

Abstract: Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA) is a key space syntax method for understanding how spatial configuration shapes human movement, but its reliance on all-pairs BFS computation limits practical application to small study areas. We present a system that combines three techniques to scale VGA to city-scale problems: (i) delta-compressed CSR storage using LEB128 varint encoding, which achieves ~4x compression and enables memory-mapped graphs exceeding available RAM; (ii) HyperBall, a probabilistic distance estimator based on HyperLogLog counter propagation, applied here for the first time to visibility graphs, reducing BFS complexity from O(N|E|) to O(D|E|2^p); and (iii) GPU-accelerated CUDA kernels with a fused decode-union kernel that streams the compressed graph via PCIe and performs LEB128 decoding entirely in shared memory. HyperBall's iteration count equals the topological depth limit, so the radius-n analysis that practitioners already use as standard translates directly into proportional speedup -- unlike depthmapX, whose BFS time is invariant to depth setting due to the small diameter of visibility graphs. Using depthmapX's own visibility algorithm (sparkSieve2) to ensure identical edge sets, our tool achieves a 239x end-to-end speedup at 42,705 cells and scales to 236,000 cells (4.8 billion edges) in 137 seconds -- problem sizes far beyond depthmapX's practical limit. At p=10, Visual Mean Depth achieves Pearson r=0.999 with 1.7% median relative error across 20 matched configurations.

</details>


### [203] [NL-CPS: Reinforcement Learning-Based Kubernetes Control Plane Placement in Multi-Region Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08434)
*Sajid Alam,Amjad Ullah,Ze Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经上下文赌博机的强化学习框架，用于智能选择多云边环境下的Kubernetes控制平面节点位置，实验表明该方法显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构多区域环境中，Kubernetes控制平面节点的放置对集群的可靠性、可扩展性和性能至关重要。然而，现有的初始化过程通常随意选择控制平面主机，未考虑节点资源容量或网络拓扑，常导致集群性能次优和弹性降低。由于Kubernetes已成为容器编排的事实标准，亟需严格评估控制平面节点放置如何影响跨多区域运行集群的整体性能。

Method: 提出了一种强化学习框架，基于神经上下文赌博机。该方法作为自动化编排系统的一部分，观测运行性能，并从基础设施特征中学习最优的控制平面放置策略，实现在跨多个区域的动态云边资源上智能选择控制平面节点位置。

Result: 在多个地理分布区域和多种集群配置下的实验评估表明，与多种基线方法相比，该方法带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能控制平面节点放置方法能够有效提升多区域云边环境下Kubernetes集群的性能，证明了利用强化学习技术优化控制平面放置的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: The placement of Kubernetes control-plane nodes is critical to ensuring cluster reliability, scalability, and performance, and therefore represents a significant deployment challenge in heterogeneous, multi-region environments. Existing initialisation procedures typically select control-plane hosts arbitrarily, without considering node resource capacity or network topology, often leading to suboptimal cluster performance and reduced resilience. Given Kubernetes's status as the de facto standard for container orchestration, there is a need to rigorously evaluate how control-plane node placement influences the overall performance of the cluster operating across multiple regions. This paper advances this goal by introducing an intelligent methodology for selecting control-plane node placement across dynamically selected Cloud-Edge resources spanning multiple regions, as part of an automated orchestration system. More specifically, we propose a reinforcement learning framework based on neural contextual bandits that observes operational performance and learns optimal control-plane placement policies from infrastructure characteristics. Experimental evaluation across several geographically distributed regions and multiple cluster configurations demonstrates substantial performance improvements over several baseline approaches.

</details>


### [204] [Taming GPU Underutilization via Static Partitioning and Fine-grained CPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08451)
*Gabin Schieffer,Ruimin Shi,Jie Ren,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对GPU多实例（MIG）共享进行系统级表征，发现虽能提升利用率和吞吐，但存在资源干扰与供给不匹配问题，并提出基于NVLink-C2C的内存卸载方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU计算能力与内存容量提升带来机遇，但多样应用导致资源利用不均，MIG作为资源分区隔离方案在HPC负载中的有效性与局限尚不明确，亟需系统评估。

Method: 使用真实科学计算、AI与数据分析应用（NekRS、LAMMPS、Llama3、Qiskit），对不同GPU共享选项进行全面的系统级表征与性能-资源扩展分析。

Result: MIG共享显著减少资源利用不足，提升系统吞吐与能效，但共享资源（如功率节流）仍导致干扰；粗粒度供给与紧密耦合的计算/内存需求常不匹配。

Conclusion: 为弥补粗粒度资源切片与实际需求差距，提出利用缓存一致性NVLink-C2C互连的内存卸载方案，以减少资源利用不足。

Abstract: Advances in GPU compute throughput and memory capacity brings significant opportunities to a wide range of workloads. However, efficiently utilizing these resources remains challenging, particularly because diverse application characteristics may result in imbalanced utilization. Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) is a promising approach to improve utilization by partitioning GPU compute and memory resources into fixed-size slices with isolation. Yet, its effectiveness and limitations in supporting HPC workloads remain an open question. We present a comprehensive system-level characterization of different GPU sharing options using real-world scientific, AI, and data analytics applications, including NekRS, LAMMPS, Llama3, and Qiskit. Our analysis reveals that while GPU sharing via MIG can significantly reduce resource underutilization, and enable system-level improvements in throughput and energy, interference still occurs through shared resources, such as power throttling. Our performance-resource scaling results indicate that coarse-grained provisioning for tightly coupled compute and memory resources often mismatches application needs. To address this mismatch, we propose a memory-offloading scheme that leverages the cache-coherent Nvlink-C2C interconnect to bridge the gap between coarse-grained resource slices and reduce resource underutilization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Position Paper: From Edge AI to Adaptive Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07360)
*Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出边缘AI在现实部署中必须具备自适应能力，引入ASE框架明确自适应要素，并提出未来十年的十个研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边缘AI被视为静态的模型压缩与部署，但在长期运行中，固定的非自适应配置面临根本性失败模式：随着数据和操作条件随时间演变，系统要么违反时变预算（延迟/能耗/热量/连接/隐私），要么失去预测可靠性（准确性和校准性），风险集中在瞬态和稀有时间区间而非平均性能中。如果部署系统无法在演进条件和约束下重新配置计算和模型状态，它将退化为静态嵌入式推理，无法提供持续效用。

Method: 引入最小化Agent-System-Environment (ASE)视角，通过明确四个关键要素使边缘自适应能力精确化：(i)什么发生变化，(ii)什么被观测，(iii)什么可以重新配置，(iv)哪些约束必须随时间保持满足。基于此框架，系统性地识别和阐述研究挑战。

Result: 提出了十个面向未来十年的核心研究挑战，涵盖：演进系统的理论保证、动态架构与数据驱动/模型基础的混合转换、故障/异常驱动的定向更新、System-1/System-2分解（即时智能）、模块化、稀缺标签下的验证、量化生命周期效率以及漂移和干预下恢复/稳定性的评估协议。

Conclusion: 边缘AI必须从静态模型压缩范式转向自适应系统范式。ASE框架为边缘自适应提供了精确的概念基础，十个研究挑战为学术界和工业界指明了未来发展方向，需要在理论保证、架构设计、验证方法和评估协议等多个层面进行系统性创新。

Abstract: Edge AI is often framed as model compression and deployment under tight constraints. We argue a stronger operational thesis: Edge AI in realistic deployments is necessarily adaptive. In long-horizon operation, a fixed (non-adaptive) configuration faces a fundamental failure mode: as data and operating conditions evolve and change in time, it must either (i) violate time-varying budgets (latency/energy/thermal/connectivity/privacy) or (ii) lose predictive reliability (accuracy and, critically, calibration), with risk concentrating in transient regimes and rare time intervals rather than in average performance. If a deployed system cannot reconfigure its computation - and, when required, its model state - under evolving conditions and constraints, it reduces to static embedded inference and cannot provide sustained utility. This position paper introduces a minimal Agent-System-Environment (ASE) lens that makes adaptivity precise at the edge by specifying (i) what changes, (ii) what is observed, (iii) what can be reconfigured, and (iv) which constraints must remain satisfied over time. Building on this framing, we formulate ten research challenges for the next decade, spanning theoretical guarantees for evolving systems, dynamic architectures and hybrid transitions between data-driven and model-based components, fault/anomaly-driven targeted updates, System-1/System-2 decompositions (anytime intelligence), modularity, validation under scarce labels, and evaluation protocols that quantify lifecycle efficiency and recovery/stability under drift and interventions.

</details>


### [206] [Self-Calibrating LLM-Based Analog Circuit Sizing with Interpretable Design Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07387)
*Antonio J. Bujana,Aydin I. Karsilayan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自校准模拟电路尺寸设计框架，直接从电路网表生成特定拓扑的分析设计方程，通过确定性校准循环和预测误差反馈机制，在12种OTA拓扑-工艺组合中实现了快速收敛（多数在2-9次仿真内满足所有规格），并具备跨工艺节点的可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI驱动电路尺寸设计方法通常依赖于模型提出参数调整建议或缩小搜索空间，缺乏从原始电路网表直接生成完整解析设计方程的能力。此外，传统方法在处理工艺相关参数变化和跨节点移植时面临挑战，需要大量重新训练或针对特定工艺的表征。因此，作者旨在开发一个能够自动推导拓扑特定设计方程、自动校准工艺参数并具备跨节点移植能力的自校准框架。

Method: 该框架利用大语言模型（LLM）直接从原始电路网表推导出特定拓扑的分析设计方程，生成一个完整的Python尺寸设计函数，将每个器件尺寸映射到特定的性能约束。通过一个确定性校准循环，从单次晶体管级仿真中提取工艺相关参数；同时引入预测误差反馈机制来补偿解析模型的误差。整个方法不依赖模型预测的初始精度，而是通过测量反馈架构确保收敛。

Result: 在跨越三个系列、两个工艺节点（180 nm和40 nm CMOS）的六种运算跨导放大器（OTA）拓扑上验证了该框架。所有12种拓扑-工艺节点组合均满足了所有设计规格，其中11种组合在2-9次仿真内收敛，一个因可行域极窄的特例需要16次仿真。尽管初始预测误差较大，但收敛性依赖于测量反馈架构而非预测精度。该方法实现了“一次性”校准，自动捕获工艺相关变化，无需修改、重新训练或针对每个工艺进行表征即可实现跨节点移植。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一个无需训练数据、可解释且自动化的模拟电路尺寸设计框架的可行性。通过结合LLM的代码生成能力与确定性校准和反馈机制，该方法实现了高效的收敛性能和优异的跨工艺节点通用性，为模拟电路设计自动化提供了一条新的、有前景的路径。

Abstract: We present a self-calibrating framework for analog circuit sizing in which a large language model (LLM) derives topology-specific analytical design equations directly from a raw circuit netlist. Unlike existing AI-driven sizing methods where the model proposes parameter adjustments or reduces a search space, the LLM produces a complete Python sizing function tracing each device dimension to a specific performance constraint. A deterministic calibration loop extracts process-dependent parameters from a single transistor-level simulation, while a prediction-error feedback mechanism compensates for analytical inaccuracies. We validate the framework on six operational transconductance amplifier (OTA) topologies spanning three families at two process nodes (180 nm and 40 nm CMOS). All 12 topology-node combinations achieve all specifications, converging in 2-9 simulations for 11 of 12 cases, with one outlier requiring 16 simulations due to an extremely narrow feasible region. Despite large initial prediction errors, convergence depends on the measurement-feedback architecture, not prediction accuracy. This one-shot calibration automatically captures process-dependent variations, enabling cross-node portability without modification, retraining, or per-process characterization.

</details>


### [207] [SHIELD: A Segmented Hierarchical Memory Architecture for Energy-Efficient LLM Inference on Edge NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07396)
*Jintao Zhang,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出SHIELD，一种生命周期感知的分段eDRAM架构，通过对BF16激活值进行符号/指数与尾数的分离，并对瞬时QO尾数禁用刷新、对持久KV尾数放宽刷新，在保持模型精度的同时减少了35%的eDRAM刷新能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘NPU上的LLM推理受限于片上内存容量。虽然高密度eDRAM适合存储激活值，但其周期性刷新消耗大量能量。现有工作主要关注减少片外流量或优化持久KV缓存的刷新，而忽视了瞬时且具有错误弹性的Query和Attention Output（QO）激活值。

Method: 提出生命周期感知的分段eDRAM架构（SHIELD）。该方法利用BF16激活值的时间驻留特性和比特级敏感度：将符号位和指数位与尾数位隔离；对瞬时的QO尾数完全禁用刷新；对持久的KV尾数采用放宽的刷新策略。

Result: 在多种LLM和推理场景下，SHIELD相比标准刷新基线减少了35%的eDRAM刷新能耗，同时在WikiText-2、PIQA和ARC-Easy基准测试中保持了模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过联合利用激活值的生命周期特性和数据格式中的比特级容错差异，SHIELD成功在边缘NPU上实现了eDRAM能效的显著提升，且未牺牲LLM推理精度，为受限环境下的LLM部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference on edge Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is fundamentally constrained by limited on-chip memory capacity. Although high-density embedded DRAM (eDRAM) is attractive for storing activation workspaces, its periodic refresh consumes substantial energy. Prior work has primarily focused on reducing off-chip traffic or optimizing refresh for persistent Key-Value (KV) caches, while transient and error-resilient Query and Attention Output (QO) activations are largely overlooked. We propose SHIELD, a lifecycle-aware segmented eDRAM architecture that jointly exploits temporal residency and bit-level sensitivity in bfloat16 (BF16) activations. SHIELD isolates the sign and exponent fields from the mantissa, disables refresh for transient QO mantissas, and applies relaxed refresh to persistent KV mantissas. Across multiple LLMs and inference scenarios, SHIELD reduces eDRAM refresh energy by 35% relative to a standard-refresh baseline while preserving accuracy on WikiText-2, PIQA, and ARC-Easy.

</details>


### [208] [FILCO: Flexible Composing Architecture with Real-Time Reconfigurability for DNN Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07523)
*Xingzhen Chen,Jinming Zhuang,Zhuoping Yang,Shixin Ji,Sarah Schultz,Zheng Dong,Weisong Shi,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FILCO灵活组合架构，通过实时重配置统一或多个独立加速器，解决异构平台中多样化工作负载的硬件资源效率匹配问题，实现1.3x-5x的吞吐量和硬件效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专用架构存在硬件与工作负载不匹配问题，而可动态切换数据流的覆盖层架构受限于灵活性粒度且导致资源效率低下，无法有效解决异构计算平台上多样化工作负载的高资源效率需求。

Method: 提出FILCO灵活组合架构，支持实时重配置并可灵活组合为统一或多个独立加速器；构建包含两阶段设计空间探索（DSE）分析模型的FILCO框架，以实现最优设计点选择。

Result: 在7nm AMD Versal VCK190板上进行评估，相比现有工作，在多样化工作负载上实现1.3x至5倍的吞吐量和硬件效率提升。

Conclusion: FILCO通过灵活组合架构和两阶段DSE框架，有效解决了异构平台上多样化工作负载的资源效率匹配问题，显著提升了吞吐量和硬件效率，为DNN加速器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: With the development of deep neural network (DNN) enabled applications, achieving high hardware resource efficiency on diverse workloads is non-trivial in heterogeneous computing platforms. Prior works discuss dedicated architectures to achieve maximal resource efficiency. However, a mismatch between hardware and workloads always exists in various diverse workloads. Other works discuss overlay architecture that can dynamically switch dataflow for different workloads. However, these works are still limited by flexibility granularity and induce much resource inefficiency.
  To solve this problem, we propose a flexible composing architecture, FILCO, that can efficiently match diverse workloads to achieve the optimal storage and computation resource efficiency. FILCO can be reconfigured in real-time and flexibly composed into a unified or multiple independent accelerators. We also propose the FILCO framework, including an analytical model with a two-stage DSE that can achieve the optimal design point. We also evaluate the FILCO framework on the 7nm AMD Versal VCK190 board. Compared with prior works, our design can achieve 1.3x - 5x throughput and hardware efficiency on various diverse workloads.

</details>


### [209] [From LLM to Silicon: RL-Driven ASIC Architecture Exploration for On-Device AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07526)
*Ravindra Ganti,Steve Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的编译器，能够在3nm至28nm工艺节点间联合优化ASIC架构、存储层次和工作负载划分，无需人工重新调优即可自动适配不同工艺节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASIC设计在不同工艺节点间迁移时，需要针对架构、存储和任务划分进行大量手动调整，设计空间巨大且优化复杂。为了实现跨工艺节点的自动化设计优化，需要一个能够联合探索架构、存储和映射策略的统一框架。

Method: 将设计空间建模为单一马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），采用混合离散-连续动作空间和统一的功耗-性能-面积（PPA）目标函数。使用带有混合专家门控机制（Mixture-of-Experts gating）的Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）算法，联合探索网格拓扑、每核微架构和算子放置的优化空间。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 8B FP16（高性能模式，3nm下29809 tokens/s）和SmolVLM（低功耗模式，所有节点功耗低于13mW，10MHz）两种工作负载上验证。覆盖7个工艺节点，RL自动调整网格规模和每tile配置（包括异构FETCH、VLEN和内存分配），无需针对特定节点进行手动重调。

Conclusion: 该RL驱动编译器成功实现了跨工艺节点的自动化设计优化，证明了强化学习方法在ASIC架构、存储层次和工作负载划分联合优化中的有效性，大幅降低了设计迁移的人工成本。

Abstract: We present an RL-driven compiler that jointly optimizes ASIC architecture, memory hierarchy, and workload partitioning for AI inference across 3nm to 28nm. The design space is formulated as a single Markov Decision Process with mixed discrete-continuous actions and a unified Power-Performance-Area (PPA) objective. Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with Mixture-of-Experts gating explores the joint space of mesh topology, per-core microarchitecture, and operator placement. We validate on two workloads, Llama 3.1 8B FP16 (high-performance mode, 29809 tokens per second at 3nm) and SmolVLM (low-power mode, less than 13 mW at all nodes, 10 MHz). Across 7 process nodes, the RL automatically adapts mesh sizes and per-tile configurations, including heterogeneous FETCH, VLEN, and memory allocation without node-specific manual retuning.

</details>


### [210] [Trilinear Compute-in-Memory Architecture for Energy-Efficient Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07628)
*Md Zesun Ahmed Mia,Jiahui Duan,Kai Ni,Abhronil Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出TrilinearCIM，一种基于双栅铁电晶体管(DG-FeFET)的存内计算架构，利用背栅调制实现三操作数乘累加原语，首次在无需运行时重新编程的情况下，完全在非易失性存储器核心中完成Transformer注意力计算，在BERT和ViT模型上实现了显著的能效和延迟改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的自注意力机制生成动态操作数，迫使传统存内计算(CIM)加速器进行昂贵的非易失性存储器(NVM)重编程周期，这会降低吞吐量并损害器件耐久性。现有解决方案要么通过矩阵分解或稀疏性减少但保留NVM写入，要么将注意力计算转移到数字CMOS但牺牲了NVM密度，存在明显的性能权衡。

Method: 提出TrilinearCIM架构，基于双栅铁电晶体管(DG-FeFET)，利用背栅调制技术实现三操作数乘累加(MAC)原语，从而在存内完成注意力计算而无需动态铁电重编程。这种设计避免了传统CIM架构中昂贵的NVM重编程周期。

Result: 在BERT-base(GLUE任务)和ViT-base(ImageNet和CIFAR)上评估，TrilinearCIM在九个GLUE任务中的七个上优于传统CIM，相比传统FeFET CIM实现了最高46.6%的能耗降低和20.4%的延迟改善，面积开销为37.3%。

Conclusion: TrilinearCIM是首个在无需运行时重编程的情况下，完全在NVM核心中执行Transformer注意力计算的架构，成功解决了动态操作数导致的NVM重编程问题，为Transformer在存内计算架构上的高效部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Self-attention in Transformers generates dynamic operands that force conventional Compute-in-Memory (CIM) accelerators into costly non-volatile memory (NVM) reprogramming cycles, degrading throughput and stressing device endurance. Existing solutions either reduce but retain NVM writes through matrix decomposition or sparsity, or move attention computation to digital CMOS at the expense of NVM density. We present TrilinearCIM, a Double-Gate FeFET (DG-FeFET)-based architecture that uses back-gate modulation to realize a three-operand multiply-accumulate primitive for in-memory attention computation without dynamic ferroelectric reprogramming. Evaluated on BERT-base (GLUE) and ViT-base (ImageNet and CIFAR), TrilinearCIM outperforms conventional CIM on seven of nine GLUE tasks while achieving up to 46.6\% energy reduction and 20.4\% latency improvement over conventional FeFET CIM at 37.3\% area overhead. To our knowledge, this is the first architecture to perform complete Transformer attention computation exclusively in NVM cores without runtime reprogramming.

</details>


### [211] [The Hyperscale Lottery: How State-Space Models Have Sacrificed Edge Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07935)
*Robin Geens,Jonas De Schouwer,Marian Verhelst,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了“超大规模彩票”现象：SSM模型（如Mamba）为追求云端GPU吞吐量而牺牲边缘端效率。研究发现Mamba-3相比Mamba-1导致边缘延迟增加28%-48%，呼吁将云规模饱和策略与核心架构设计解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受“硬件彩票”假说启发，作者识别出一种衍生现象——“超大规模彩票”：模型架构针对云端吞吐量进行优化，却牺牲了算法效率。尽管Mamba等状态空间模型（SSM）因线性复杂性被视为边缘智能的理想选择，但从Mamba-1到Mamba-3的演进显示出系统性地偏离了边缘原生效率。

Method: 研究分析了从Mamba-1到Mamba-3的架构演变，识别出为饱和超大规模GPU而设计的架构变化。通过对比实验，测量了不同参数规模模型（880M参数和15M参数）在边缘环境下的延迟表现。

Result: Mamba-3的架构变化对边缘端施加了显著惩罚：在880M参数规模下延迟增加28%，在15M参数的小模型中更是恶化至48%的延迟增加。这证明了针对云端优化的设计决策严重损害了边缘推理性能。

Conclusion: 作者主张将云规模饱和策略与核心架构设计解耦，以保护单用户、实时边缘智能的可行性，避免因过度追求云端性能而牺牲边缘端效率。

Abstract: The Hardware Lottery posits that research directions are dictated by available silicon compute platforms. We identify a derivative phenomenon, the Hyperscale Lottery, where model architectures are optimized for cloud throughput at the expense of algorithmic efficiency. While State-Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba were lauded for their linear complexity, ideal for edge intelligence, their evolution from Mamba-1 to Mamba-3 reveals a systematic divergence from edge-native efficiency. We demonstrate that Mamba-3's architectural changes, designed to saturate hyperscale GPUs, impose a significant edge penalty: a 28% latency increase at 880M parameters, worsening to 48% for 15M-parameter models. We argue for decoupling cloud-scale saturation strategies from core architectural design to preserve the viability of single-user, real-time edge intelligence.

</details>


### [212] [A Full-Stack Performance Evaluation Infrastructure for 3D-DRAM-based LLM Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08044)
*Cong Li,Chenhao Xue,Yi Ren,Xiping Dong,Yu Cheng,Yinbo Hu,Fujun Bai,Yixin Guo,Xiping Jiang,Qiang Wu,Zhi Yang,Zhe Cheng,Yuan Xie,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了ATLAS，首个基于商用多层3D-DRAM技术的硅验证LLM加速器仿真框架，解决了现有3D-DRAM加速器缺乏公开性能分析工具和通用建模方法的问题，仿真误差≤8.57%，将与开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型解码过程存在内存密集行为，是推理的关键瓶颈。虽然混合键合3D-DRAM已用于LLM加速器，但现有3D-DRAM加速器依赖闭源评估工具，缺乏公开的性能分析方法，且设计高度定制化，缺乏跨场景的通用可复用全栈建模。

Method: 基于商业部署的多层3D-DRAM技术，构建ATLAS仿真框架。引入统一的抽象机制，涵盖3D加速器系统架构和编程原语，以支持任意LLM推理场景，实现全栈建模。

Result: 与真实硅片验证对比，ATLAS实现了≤8.57%的仿真误差和97.26-99.96%的性能相关性。通过设计空间探索，证明了其能够指导架构设计，并提炼出跨场景下3D-DRAM内存系统和微架构的关键见解。

Conclusion: ATLAS填补了3D-DRAM加速器缺乏通用仿真工具的空白，为架构设计提供了可靠的分析平台，将开源发布以促进该领域进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit memory-intensive behavior during decoding, making it a key bottleneck in LLM inference. To accelerate decoding execution, hybrid-bonding-based 3D-DRAM has been adopted in LLM accelerators. While this emerging technology provides strong performance gains over existing hardware, current 3D-DRAM accelerators (3D-Accelerators) rely on closed-source evaluation tools, limiting access to publicly available performance analysis methods. Moreover, existing designs are highly customized for specific scenarios, lacking a general and reusable full-stack modeling for 3D-Accelerators across diverse usecases.
  To bridge this fundamental gap, we present ATLAS, the first silicon-proven Architectural Three-dimesional-DRAM-based LLM Accelerator Simulation framework. Built on commercially deployed multi-layer 3D-DRAM technology, ATLAS introduces unified abstractions for both 3D-Accelerator system architecture and programming primitives to support arbitrary LLM inference scenarios. Validation against real silicon shows that ATLAS achieves $\le$8.57% simulation error and 97.26-99.96\% correlation with measured performance. Through design space exploration with ATLAS, we demonstrate its ability to guide architecture design and distill key takeaways for both 3D-DRAM memory system and 3D-Accelerator microarchitecture across scenarios. ATLAS will be open-sourced upon publication, enabling further research on 3D-Accelerators.

</details>
